([sue-btis/ChangeSmith](https://github.com/sue-btis/ChangeSmith/tree/HEAD/compact-change-skills/skills/writing-change-plan))
- **writing-change-records**: Writes commit messages, pull request descriptions, issues and tickets, progress comments on issues, the text of review comments on someone else's PR or document, and replies to review comments on your own PR. Use when drafting or updating any of these, including a PR description before opening a PR, a bug report, a ticket for another team, or a reply to reviewer or bot feedback. To decide what to say about the prose of someone's document, use reviewing-prose first. ([jml/dotfiles](https://github.com/jml/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/plugins/technical-writing/skills/writing-change-records) · ★4)
- **writing-change-spec**: Write Change Spec. Produce a proportional implementation-ready specification for quick, medium, or large software changes. Ground it in the evidence facts or the audit, freeze only approved decisions, assign stable requirement and acceptance IDs, preserve provenance, and self-review the same spec before approval. ([sue-btis/ChangeSmith](https://github.com/sue-btis/ChangeSmith/tree/HEAD/compact-change-skills/skills/writing-change-spec))
- **writing-changelog**: Use when a Google Analytics for WooCommerce pull request needs a changelog entry, or when deciding whether it needs one. Covers the "### Changelog entry" section of the PR template and the "changelog: none" label. Triggers on "add a changelog entry", "does this need a changelog", "write the changelog".
